Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors include: sample3.exe execution with PID 4834, ADS append to win.ini:secret_log, keyboard hook (SetWindowsHookEx), Run key persistence, no network activity. Unique defects: 1. Missing core behavior (Run key persistence in student-2, student-5); 2. Incorrect PID (student-2, student-3, student-4); 3. Wrong hash (student-0, student-7); 4. Time discrepancies (student-0, student-3, student-7); 5. Placeholder in 'Khác' (student-4); 6. Unresolved evidence reference (student-3's ACCESS DENIED attributed to Windows Defender not in golden).
